# Culmina finalization review

**2026-10-18 — P1–P8 baseline complete; owner amendments AM-01/AM-02 incorporated as roadmap P6-2026-10-18-04. §33 records the five newly accepted actions and the Claude assessment prompt. Implementation still requires a separate start.**

Current evidence and decisions are in §14–§33: P2 host observations, P3 proposed specifications/sketches, P4 independent Codex feasibility review, P5 owner decision package, P6 finalization (begun by Codex, completed by Claude after Codex's usage limit), the P7 check and the P8 approval record. P1's earlier statements about checks not run apply to P1 only. No phase marks the proposed implementation, physical-phone acceptance or owner decisions complete.

Authority: the owner-invoked `CULMINA_FINALIZATION_WORKFLOW_AND_PROMPTS_UK.md`, P0/P1, identified by its original filename (external provenance only, not a project dependency). Workflow SHA-256: `AEC4D50303AEFE4E3DF98B5A89953B42E51D95F3E716296E3BEDB2D27B51F370`.

This is a working review, not another execution queue, owner approval, application validation or independent Claude review. P1 permits only this report and explicitly ends after it; no roadmap rewrite, application implementation, commit or push is included. Later phases remain pending. Invoking the workflow does not choose D1–D9 or supply its distinct approval/start/publication instructions.

## 2. Coverage interpretation and sources

Each matrix row is a disposition, not evidence that its future test passed. IMPLEMENT/VERIFY refer to remaining work in the still-proposed F scope. MERGE retains an outcome through a named criterion. COMPLETE uses dated repository records. EXCLUDE retains a stated decision. OWNER DECISION stays unresolved. `proposed A+` identifies insufficiently explicit acceptance, not a silently approved new criterion.

CODE means current source inspected; HISTORICAL/REUSED means dated repository evidence, not a rerun. No TEST EXECUTED, RENDER, DEVICE or HUMAN OBSERVATION was newly collected in P1. Runtime absence stays UNVERIFIED. Test-file existence is not execution. Checks `CHK-Fxx`/`CHK-Gx` refer to the concrete verification allocation in §9; the source rows name the specific criterion/outcome to check.

| OLD §5 / S14.1 contract | **ADR-021 "Capture Plan completeness"**, the technical design of PD-05 to PD-14 and PD-21; The ADR records:; • the white-balance model;; • per-field value sources and their persistence;; • the storage evidence chain, with the **binning-in-storage rule and its vectors**, and whether a block's measured file size is persisted (readiness review C3, 2026-10-17);; • the metadata contract extension, with the tags and their provenance;; • the FITS keywords and limits;; • CAL-1's rules;; • O2b's axis and normalisation;; • the v26 schema design.; It names its amendments: ADR-009 §7/L6, ADR-017, ADR-018 D4, ADR-020 §2/§3/§5/§10, SI-013, CALC-16, CALC-42, and the superseded Stage 11 storage row. **The owner accepts the ADR** (the binning rule and the persistence choice are the technical points to confirm); **Audit clarification:** Freeze one contract for the already approved metadata, per-field origins, storage and calibration paths before v26: field types/units/destinations and unsupported/conflict states; mode-scoped measured size and theoretical payload; calibration tuple matching versus proven duplicate coverage; O2b group comparability and zero-integration behaviour. ADR-021 lists, per required rig field, the calculations that need it and whether the editor or a photo can supply it; the editor shows that reason beside each required field (AUD-04; audit 09 M4 (d)). This amendment does not approve a partial-rig mode, nullable-rig migration, a spec service or a new equipment model; any change to ADR-011's required-input invariant must be explicitly decided when the owner accepts ADR-021. Legacy values without a source remain 'origin unrecorded'. Reuse existing services and the already approved FITS reader; no second metadata pipeline or new scientific model. AUD-02/04/05/06/16.; **Audit 09 (owner-approved 2026-10-17):** The ADR also records: (a) the FITS → rig mapping (XPIXSZ/YPIXSZ, FOCALLEN, TELESCOP) and its binning normalisation for binned and unbinned writers (CA-06, M5); (b) whether the length of a picked file whose format is recognised but not read (CR2, CR3, NEF, ARW, RAF, RW2, ORF, XISF, AVIF, PNG) is measured storage evidence (CA-07, M6); (c) CAL-1 filter identity: names compared after trimming and case folding, no alias guessing — `Ha` equals `HA`; `H-alpha` does not equal `Ha` and is reported, not merged (CA-03, M3); (d) the owner question **CA-OD3**: whether S14.3 adds 'Undo the last restore', and how many safety copies are kept. It is presented as a numbered decision sheet, one recommendation per point (CA-22). At S14.1's start the owner is asked for the FITS sample (an owner file or a licensed public fixture), so the gate is known before S14.4 (CA-15, M14). | Approved source; F proposal pending | DOCUMENTED; future runtime/acceptance UNVERIFIED | MERGE / IMPLEMENT | F03 A1/A5; F04 A1-A4/F07 A1-A5/F09 A1-A4/F10 A1-A4/F11 A1-A3 | CHK-F03, CHK-F04, CHK-F07, CHK-F09, CHK-F10, CHK-F11 | Preserve outcome; A+ is a partial. Scope classification: existing-defect corrections include F01/F02/F06/F09. Previously approved-undelivered WB/calendar/overview/CSV/photo/calibration remain scope through PD-09/PD-17/PD-16/Q4/PD-11/PD-12. “No new features” does not discard them. New unapproved optional R/N/CA proposals stay separate. Absolute physical SNR and a numeric focus slider remain excluded.

RA.R1 contradiction aliases: K1/K2 → historical report/path/current-state reconciliation; K3 → S12.1/G1/F26; K4 → Q1/F14/F17/F18; K5 → F11; K6 → F07; K7 → F06/F12; K8 → F09; K9 → F17; K10 → completed identity; K11 → RD-09/F19/COV-01; K12 → F12 A1/A4; K13 stays historical, not a new task; K14 → U/W/F25 and accepted S6.E limitation. Later references to “K16” do not create a new RA.R1 contradiction: the source table has K1–K14; preserve the actual observation instead.

### Delivery gate checks

| Check | Finite required evidence | Boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CHK-G1 | frozen F/Stage11 rows; V3 input diff; full gate, actual remote CI, native compile/Kotlin, affected emulator lifecycle/backup/offline/permissions and live providers/links | F10 complete or explicit PD-11/RG-03 deferral; mandatory device rows assigned G2 |
| CHK-G2 | G2.1 core loop/W1-W4; .2 real metadata; .3 physical accessibility/field/motion/perf/startup; .4 restore+interruption+cross-id/schema content AND UI; .5 actual signed clean install/same-key update; .6 authorized owner migration or owner-confirmed N/A | actual selected APK SHA256/signer; fixtures/test package rehearsal distinct from production artifact |
| CHK-G3 | mandatory-row reconciliation, no severe defect, explicit optional/accepted limitations, owner real-night dogfooding and go/no-go | not a new comprehensive audit; S6.E accepted limitation does not waive other person/device rows |
| CHK-G4 | authorized tag/release on correct commit, exact tested artifacts, published download hash/public signer equality, final phone core loop, README/PROGRESS URLs | no rebuild; publication pending is not roadmap complete |

Dependency conclusion: intended F core chains and G1 → F26 → G2 → G3 → G4 are acyclic. COV-03 must remove the literal F18/F10 ambiguity. These are expected paths derived from existing approved outcomes and the proposed amendments. D8/D9-dependent steps remain proposals until accepted. They are not completed walkthroughs.

| W2 DSLR | existing rig → light photo proposal → inspect/edit source → Save; second attempt Cancel → calibration preview/confirm → Save/reopen | unread RAW honest result (storage only per D3); type switch flat/bias; custom filter; dirty Back guard | real-file vs fixture evidence; source/value/cancel persistence comparison |

| W4 Returning | saved plan → inspect original result/snapshot → browse another night → Back → explicit Plan this night/Copy → Save new plan → record result → Logbook/progress → Plan this target; find New plan in Planner menu | dirty Save/Discard/Cancel; restart/context change; original snapshot/forecast/result unchanged; stored-record Delete confirmation | D9 state labelled proposed until accepted; contents plus derived UI; no builder comprehension PASS |

Also cover Welcome/Home/Library/Settings and isolated backup/restore. Choose risk combinations rather than a Cartesian product: long editor + 200% + keyboard; saved plan + browse + Back/restart; stale forecast + changed night/site; fixture restore + interrupted activation. U supplies 412 px, 100/200%, light/dark/field and applicable semantics/motion requirements; smaller actually supported screens where available. No invented time/tap quota. Reproduction command, from the repository root with the generated ignored probe present:

```powershell
$env:FLUTTER_ROOT = 'C:\flutter'
& 'C:\flutter\bin\flutter.bat' test --no-pub --reporter expanded build/finalization/p2_observation_test.dart
```

The patch records the exact executed source at `build/finalization/p2_observation_test.dart` (SHA-256 `e6c96fe27fb0ba8fe59aef125013f636b171693fb5be29163bb8c2126bb296d6`). On a clean validation checkout it can recreate that ignored file with `git apply`; do not apply it over an existing file blindly. It is an assessment probe, not a permanent app regression test or an implementation change. Its location follows P2's restriction against editing the normal production/test tree; the source is preserved as a probe `.patch` under docs. The widget-test skill supplied interaction methods, not authority to modify app code.

Harness-development logs retain an initial API-name compile error, one manually interrupted fake/real event-loop stall, and lazy-list finder corrections. These are **probe failures**, not Culmina defects. The final source fixes the orchestration by pumping both zones and scrolling lazy lists. Final execution is the successful `host-observation.log`; earlier attempt logs are not acceptance evidence. Inspection contact sheets were generated only under ignored `build/finalization/inspection/`; unchanged full-resolution PNGs and their hashes are the durable evidence.

### Reference-plan density record

The fixture contains three light blocks L/Ha/OIII (60 s ×100 each), plus dark (60 s ×20), flat L (2 s ×20) and bias (0.001 s ×20), a DSLR rig with unknown tracking, ordinary default disclosure state. Current tracking warnings therefore repeat per light. No artificial maximum scroll length is introduced.

| Measurement | Result / interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| Capture Plan section rendered height, dark/100%, 412×915 | **1,444 logical px** |
| Whole Planner maximum scroll extent after reaching the actual bottom | **3,045 logical px**, stable for three iterations; [bottom capture](evidence/finalization/p2/reference_plan_bottom_dark_100.png) |
| Intermediate estimates | The raw initial/partly scrolled lazy-list estimates (for example 2,333 or 5,825 px) are **not** total-content measurements and must not be used as the baseline |
| Remaining M11 evidence | No post-change section delta or owner density acceptance exists yet. Preserve this exact fixture/font/theme/disclosure/tracking context when measuring future changes. This record is not a UI length quota |

## 18. P2 exit and next allowed preparation

The bounded route/workflow inventory has an observation or explicit UNVERIFIED disposition for every row. Findings P2-F01–F14 map to existing F tasks/criteria; no new capability or global research cycle is introduced. Current screenshots, probe and structured evidence make P3 concrete. No production code, normal test/tooling input, roadmap, ADR/DECISIONS or PROGRESS was changed by P2.

**Next: P3 concrete before→after specifications and proposed sketches** for Home, Capture Plan, night conditions and long editors; include D7/D8/D9 flows and a KEEP/SIMPLIFY/MOVE/MERGE/removal disposition table. Retain calculations/provenance/saved data and current tokens pending D1. Do not ask the owner to approve unseen visuals. P4 separate Claude feasibility and P5 owner decisions remain pending; P6/approval/implementation/release have not started.

P2 verification: final two observation tests passed; actual toolchain checked; 71 PNGs/77 structured records inventoried, glyph families/zero recorded capture exceptions checked; source patch matches executed source; manifest records hashes; local report/artifact links and whitespace checked after writing. Device/human/real-file/native-IME and complete U/W acceptance stay explicitly pending. No full quality gate, current remote CI or provider check is claimed.

## 19. P3 concrete proposal baseline and visual evidence

**Status: PROPOSED specifications, not accepted behavior or application implementation.** This closes P3's deliverable: specific before→after layouts, states, entry/return contracts and simplification dispositions. It does not freeze the roadmap or approve D1–D9/optional refinements. The authoritative current app remains the P2 baseline; the proposed screens below are separately labelled.

62 static Flutter sketches use the existing neutral AppTheme/AppPalette, AppSpacing, Material controls, actual StatusBlock/CollapsibleSection and shared unsaved-change prompt, SDK Roboto and MaterialIcons. They cover Home, Capture Plan, Night/Moon, long block/rig editors, Welcome, checklist, calibration preview, forecasts, restore states, adoption, assumptions and records. The four major surfaces have light/dark/field and 100/200% examples; remaining named states have explicit variants. No new brand treatment is applied while D1 is unresolved.

Each sketch has a PROPOSAL caption. All dates, durations, sources and values are **illustrative layout fixtures**, not output from a new Moon/weather/capture implementation. The month repeats sample values to test layout; it is not a predicted calendar. A connected R-O2 illustration is not scientific validation. Production must consume validated domain outputs and retain unknown/no-dark/coverage distinctions. Prototype buttons and read-only fields do not persist or exercise the proposed production paths.

## 20. P3 before→after specifications

The SP IDs identify proposal sections in this report, not new execution tasks. Their A+ clauses are candidates for incorporation into the existing F criteria during P6. Existing F IDs, approved outcomes and exclusions remain. The core choice is progressive disclosure around user decisions, retaining useful calculations and provenance.

### SP01 — Optional setup and the first useful action

**Sources:** P2-F01, PD-02, OLD S16.1; F16 with F05/D8. **Before:** three steps, long permission text and Skip/Done. **After:** one page, title “Welcome to Culmina”; short purpose sentence; optional site; optional camera class → labelled rig creation/selection; one “Start planning”. No target step or Skip. Both omissions still enter planning. Only the explicit GPS tap requests permission; denial stays local with choose/manual path. Missing specifications are named, never filled with examples. New-install example class follows cited seed evidence; existing rows keep their class.

Main action remains reachable while setup content scrolls. Back/cancel from a child returns to the setup with confirmed selections retained and unsaved child edits unchanged. D8 governs whether creating a rig selects it. Startup must load the first-run state before route choice and must not wait for weather. The sketches do not replace the required first-frame test.

**Actual owners:** WelcomeScreen, AppRouter.router, Startup/Tonight/Site/SessionPlan ViewModels, first-run/display repositories and the existing equipment flows. **SP01.A+:** optional/no-target/no-Skip/short-rationale/title outcome explicitly in F16's A-list; first/returning/permission/cancel/D8 and real-font U checks.

### SP02 — Home answers for the plan's own night

**Owner amendment AM-03 (2026-10-18):** Home text that describes the plan's night uses the shared plan-night phrase: “tonight” only for the current night, otherwise the night's date; this includes the night card and the candidate action and screen, whose list is for the plan's night. By owner choice AM03-T = B (roadmap §5, decided 2026-10-18) the tab label stays “Tonight” and the page title reads “Tonight” for the current night and the plan's night otherwise (for example “Night of Fri, Dec 18”); this replaces the fixed “Tonight” title drawn below.

**Sources:** P2-F02, PD-03/Q1, OLD S16.2; F17, D9. **Before:** an empty app-bar area, prominent framed context, plan before conditions and duplicate New plan. **After:** wrapping Tonight title with field toggle on the same title row; compact tappable site/night plus zone; one night card; Your plan/Open planner; candidates as secondary action. Remove only Home's New plan action; keep Planner's menu action with its distinct new-draft lifecycle.

The night card leads with dark interval, phase/moon-free interval, dark-span cloud/coverage/freshness. Retrieval age and lead time remain different labels. Wind/gust/dew stay secondary/detail; this is not a score or physical observing verdict. No site, no dark interval, unavailable/partial/stale/offline weather keep explicit reasons and existing retry/detail paths. Weather absence does not hide the valid plan or offline Moon results. Under recommended D9 the card and Your plan always share the active plan's site/night. Do not display a browsed-night forecast beside an unlabeled different-night plan.

**Owners:** TonightHomeScreen, shared ContextLine/NightText/QuantityText, existing plan/night VMs. **SP02.A+:** F17 explicitly checks title/field toggle, compact context, secondary candidates, no Home New plan, loading/empty/error/offline and same-plan Open. Check current and saved/dirty picker Cancel; U/W1/W4. D9 adoption is SP08, not a second Home plan store.

### SP03 — Planner answer and Capture Plan without repeated context

**Owner amendment AM-03 (2026-10-18):** the status and fit actions, per-block what-fits rows, reasons and conditions text use the shared plan-night phrase; a plan for another night never reads “tonight”. Calculations and displayed values are unchanged.

**Owner amendment AM-02 (2026-10-18):** AM-02 keeps Save plan visible after load with an actionable readiness reason and existing save invariants; no hidden button for missing target/rig/site. Selected-plan-night facts lead; move Altitude now into explicitly labelled secondary detail when the planned night differs. No new partial-rig/save model.

**Owner amendment AM-01 (2026-10-18):** AM-01 supersedes the compact Tonight for this target chart requirement: remove that Plan block/placeholder for this release. Keep its domain window/fit calculations and compact availability/reason/detail navigation. Capture Plan leads with frame rows and four concise outputs: integration, estimated total duration, estimated storage, and relative gain with a stated baseline. Detailed breakdowns/charts/calibration explanations are secondary; manual count/exposure entry must not require opening them. Advanced science remains accessible and stored unchanged.

**Sources:** P2-F03/F10/F14, PD-04, M11, UXA-C5; F18/F06/F09/F25. **Before:** repeated missing target and long blocks with repeated tracking actions; example is the main labelled empty-state action. **After:** missing site/target/rig/capture checklist with each item opening its real selector; once computable, one result block with fit, needed/usable time, integration, capture end and actionable limiting reason. Keep valid partial calculations with specific Unknowns. Target appears once; AM-01 removes Tonight for this target and its chart/placeholder from Plan while retaining a compact fit/availability reason and detail routes. Remove duplicated weather row; the existing calculation remains available.

Inside the existing Capture Plan section, each block gives its type/filter, exposure/count, integration and short sensitivity/source summary; edit/delete/reorder remain accessible. A plan-wide identical tracking warning/action may be merged into one visible contextual warning, while differing block-specific limits/conflicts remain attached to their block or explicitly named in the visible warning. It must not become a blanket “all blocks safe” message. Keep displayed integration and relative-comparison explanation, source/Unknown/capability warnings and effective constraints available without tooltips alone. Full budget/storage/WB/origin details live one disclosure level down. No source/data/calculation is removed.

Empty state: “Start with light frames”, labelled “Add light frames” and class-supported “Take from a photo”, then “Build calibration from lights afterwards”. Do not remove the example until both photo/calibration replacements and replacement E2E work. Retain ExampleCapturePlan as fixture. Existing save eligibility/lifecycle remains; this does not invent a new requirement that an empty plan can never be saved. Save success clears autosave banner; failure preserves the recoverable draft.

**Owners:** HomeScreen/SessionPlannerRoute/PlanStatus, CapturePlanWidget, SessionPlan/CaptureAnalysis VMs, unchanged FitAnalyzer/budget/current-session services. **SP03.A+:** F18 names target-once/AM-01 Plan-chart removal/weather outcomes; F09 A6 labels empty entries; F06/F18/F25 require the same P2 reference fixture and section deltas, not an arbitrary maximum length. The isolated sketch is not a new route or measured production improvement.

### SP04 — Rig creation completes its invoking intent

**Sources:** P2-F04/F05, PD-07, M2, UXA-C2/C4, AUD-04; F05/F16, D8. Ask camera class first, with “Not sure” as explicit Unknown; never infer it. Expose From a photo / Enter by hand as equally visible labelled choices. Phone/DSLR photo first; Astro manual first until FITS mapping is available. Final supported builds may name FITS; recognized-only formats must not be advertised as parsed. Unknown ordering is a proposed local copy/layout choice, not a new camera mode.

Each required field states the calculation that needs it and whether it came from the file, an existing rig, estimation or user input. “Not in this file” identifies the missing value and offers another original file or manual entry; no fake sensor defaults. Keep the original completeness invariant unless explicitly amended by ADR-021. An invalid incomplete form does not save a guessed rig.

**D8 recommended:** Save from a missing-rig/onboarding path creates and selects that rig for the invoking plan, returns to the caller, clears the missing-rig state and reports “Rig saved and chosen for this plan”. Library Save reports “Rig saved” only. **Alternative:** return to the chooser with “New rig” highlighted; selection requires an explicit tap. Cancel writes/selects nothing under either option. Dirty Back/tap-outside uses the established discard guard; unchanged closes immediately.

**Owners:** EquipmentSelectionScreen/MetadataImportScreen/showEquipmentEditor, GearViewModel, EquipmentDraft/Candidate/matcher, DriftEquipmentRepository; invoking SessionPlanViewModel selection. **SP04.A+:** exact entry-return-message/Library/cancel matrix in F05 A5/F16 A3; required-field reasons/new-install sourced class/Not sure in explicit F05 A criteria. No nullable-rig/schema workaround.

### SP05 — Long block editor, source review and stable values

**Owner amendment AM-04 (2026-10-18), superseding the affected AM-01 hierarchy below:** Phone/DSLR basic entry shows exposure, count, ISO, WB and optional AF/MF, plus applicable type/filter. ISO/WB/focus remain optional; exposure supports fractions and decimal seconds. Explicit rig class selects ISO/gain; Unknown has a class remedy or unrecorded value. Binning is applicable astro Details. Preserve hidden history and source/conflict reasons. Focus is descriptive unrecorded/AF/MF with round trips, not numeric focus, hardware control or calibration identity. See F03/F06/F09/F12 and §37.

**Owner amendment AM-02 (2026-10-18):** AM-02 labels local creation Add block and editing Apply changes, distinct from parent Save plan. Existing autosave/Undo/Cancel remain; the full-screen editor has a reachable Apply changes action above the IME. No extra confirmation or parent Save is implied.

**Owner amendment AM-01 (2026-10-18):** AM-01 basic editor hierarchy: applicable frame type, exposure, count and filter first; optional class-specific settings and source/WB/storage disclosure in Details without erasing hidden values. Preserve genuine validation/Unknown/conflict reasons, photo/manual proposals and keyboard-reachable Save. This is a simpler interface over existing domain rules, not removal of calculations or a new partial-rig model.

**Sources:** P2-F06/F07/F10, PD-08/09/11, TD-108/RA-F05, UXA-C4; F03/F04/F06/F08. Recommend the existing site-editor **full-screen route pattern** for the expanded rig/block editors: wrapping title, scrollable form and one Save kept above the keyboard inset. This is a layout recommendation; an AlertDialog may remain only if it meets every existing U/guard requirement. The sketch does not prove the present dialog cannot meet U.

Order: frame/class context → Take from a photo when supported → filter → exposure/count → class-appropriate ISO or gain/binning → WB record for applicable classes → storage/source details. A custom valid filter remains visible as its selected value, not coerced to None. New flat/bias never inherit a light's exposure accidentally. Flat exposure is explicitly set for its illumination; calibration may inherit only the approved applicable fields. Sources remain per field; editing one source does not erase another. Imported/repeated proposals show conflicts inline with keep/review choices, preserve deterministic mode/pixel-size handling and write only on Save. Inline layout from R6 is a proposed refinement, not approval of a second import pipeline.

WB: Auto/preset/Kelvin/Custom with optional tint; JPEG/HEIC Auto/Manual and DNG neutral vector stay distinct records. No Kelvin guessed from a neutral vector. Missing legacy WB stays unrecorded; no change to exposure, budget, storage or color reproducibility claims. Astro-camera WB controls remain hidden under the accepted class contract. AM-04 adds optional descriptive AF/MF recording alongside the focus reminder; never a numeric field or hardware control.

Dirty Back/tap outside uses the existing discard guard with prompt dismissal = Cancel; a pending photo proposal counts as dirty. Explicit Cancel cancels the local editor/proposal without persisting it; it is never implicit Save. Save validates the existing domain bounds; errors identify their field and preserve typed input. Unchanged Back closes immediately. Save result is reported once. Source, measured-size/mode and WB survive all approved operations and snapshots/export/backup; §21 CP defines the proposed data association.

**Owners:** showCaptureBlockDialog/_CaptureBlockDialogState, EquipmentDraft/editor, current file-access/registry/import VM, CaptureBlock and SessionPlan/CurrentSession/repositories. **SP05.A+:** preserve custom/null values through unchanged and Use other values, full source operation matrix, WB round trips/calculation invariance, proposal cancellation/type switch and U keyboard traversal. The new sketch's fixed Save geometry is not production acceptance.

### SP06 — Calibration from lights is a visible reviewed action

**Sources:** P2-F08/F14, PD-12/13, TD-107, M3; F09. Place “Build calibration from lights” in Capture Plan once lights exist; before lights, the empty state explains why it follows light entry. A warning may link to this same preview as an optional R8 shortcut. No new calibration library/workflow is created.

The preview names each distinct whole modeled requirement and its source: dark exposure/sensitivity/binning; flats applicable filter/binning; class-appropriate bias/dark flats. Counts are editable proposals, not universal recommendations. Flat exposure comes from flat-specific evidence/user input, not light exposure. Missing domain-required values must be completed before a block is constructed; unknowns permitted by the existing domain remain unknown and cannot prove duplicate coverage. Keep LENR behavior, timing/library policy and non-integration semantics. Do not duplicate only when existing full coverage is proven; permissive advisory matching is not proof.

Preview Cancel/Back writes nothing; confirm adds domain-valid reviewed blocks and existing edit Undo; Undo after a newer edit preserves it. Any include-switch redesign from R8 remains optional; the sketch simply shows reviewed proposal fields and the confirm action.

**Owners:** CapturePlanWidget, CalibrationMatch, block editor/SessionPlan, current persistence/Undo. **SP06.A+:** F09 action entry and unresolved-field state become explicit criteria; whole-tuple/case/unknown/class-hidden-binning/LENR tests, preview/cancel/confirm/intervening-edit Undo and replacement E2E. Do not replace calibration advice with a claim of certified library suitability.

### SP07 — Factual night overview and readable details

**Owner amendment AM-01 (2026-10-18):** AM-01 makes the selected-night day/twilight/dark/Moon timeline and time slider the primary explanation; moving the cursor changes viewing state only. Show local selected time/date/zone, darkness/Moon state and the relevant interval end/remaining time; Now is offered only within that viewed night. Weather uses selected-night hourly cloud, temperature/dew-point graphics, compact wind/gust/precip rows and exact selected-hour values. Gaps remain gaps. Full tables/model/age and lower cloud layers/humidity stay reachable. Calendar/week/legend/text/field behavior remains. No playback/simulator, new engine/provider, UV/AQI dashboard or fabricated certainty.

**Sources:** P2-F11, PD-16/17/18, AUD-07/08, M8; F13/F14/F15. Monthly calendar, selected-night answer and combined timeline share one accepted Moon predicate. Phase name/visual comes from phase longitude; moon-free intervals come from dark ∩ Moon-below, not phase. Large text switches month grid to a readable week presentation; do not squeeze 200% labels into the same cells. Explicit selected-day labels and text alternatives accompany any glyph/color; field mode uses existing red tokens and hatching. Static repeated dates/values are sketch fixtures only.

Night cards lead with cloud in darkness and moon-free dark time, then precipitation/wind/gust/dew. Show actual per-variable coverage, forecast lead time and retrieval age separately. Missing data is not clear/zero weather; no-dark is its own known state; out-of-range, stale and offline-cached say what is available. Details keep cloud layers, wind/gusts, humidity, dew point and timing semantics. Seeing/transparency is explicitly absent with a user-opened Clear Outside link. No new provider, score, threshold or reliability percentage.

**Owners:** NightMoonScreen/WeatherDetailScreen/WeatherForecastWidget, existing Moon/NightWeather services/summary/cache/repository. **SP07.A+:** F15 explicitly names month/offline/isolate/cache/week/bar/legend/text/hatching; F14 names card hierarchy/lead/coverage/old cache migration/race safeguards. Tests use ADR-022 vectors; real provider/phone timing remains G1/G2.

### SP08 — D9 browsing and adopting are separate actions

**Sources:** UXA-C3/AUD-09; F13 A5, F14 A6, F15 A2/A3, F17 A3, F18 A3. **Recommended D9:** Home/Planner site/night context controls retain their current plan-edit meaning. Opening a picker and cancelling changes nothing; confirming uses the established saved/draft edit behavior. Do not insert Save/Discard/Cancel before every ordinary field edit: the existing guard applies when replacing/leaving the current plan.

Calendar days, month navigation and forecast-card “View this night” only change a transient viewing context. Show both contexts: “Viewing Thu, Dec 17 · Your plan: Tue, Dec 15”. Opening/back/restart without adoption never rewrites the stored plan/result. Only “Plan this night” adopts. A never-saved current plan moves as an explicit edit; a saved source uses existing Copy to another night and becomes a new unsaved copy. A current unsaved-change replacement first uses askBeforeLeavingPlan; Save failure and Cancel stop the operation. Discard follows the existing saved-snapshot restoration rule, then the explicit copy/adopt intent proceeds. Adoption/copy itself does not retime or rewrite the original saved snapshot/forecast/result. An explicit Save in the existing pre-copy guard has its ordinary approved effect; compare the copy against the source **after** that explicit Save, rather than claiming that Save performed no write. Discard copies the restored saved version; Cancel retains the prior saved version and the working edits without adoption. Report “Plan night changed to …” or “Copied to …; original saved plan unchanged” according to the actual path.

**Material alternative D9:** Home subtitle also browses. Then Home must label the viewed night and Your plan's different night separately; Planner alone edits plan context or Plan this night adopts. This is a real context/simplicity tradeoff, not two hidden behaviors for the same gesture. Neither option creates a second persistent plan store. Persisting a separate browsing context or restoring one is a local route-state detail, never authority to modify a saved plan.

**Owners:** ContextLine callbacks, NightMoon/Weather overview, AppRouter, PlanLifecycleViewModel/SessionPlanViewModel, askBeforeLeavingPlan and existing repository copy. **SP08.A+:** one table per control and saved/dirty/draft state in ADR-022; test original contents plus derived UI after browse/back/Cancel/restart/site change, save error and adoption. Current sketch tests only the shared prompt Cancel; it does not execute this future workflow.

### SP09 — D7 restore activation and truthful outcomes

**Sources:** P2-F09, F01 A1–A5/G2.4, D2/D7, UXA-C1. Candidate is validated at pick time before confirmation and again before activation via the existing production connection on an isolated copy; this is separate from UI styling. Confirmation names backup date/schema/version/count with correct singular/plural and recommends an independent backup. It promises only accessible recovery. No safety-copy Undo is advertised unless D2 explicitly enables it.

**Recommended D7:** “Restore and restart” starts a non-editable activation/restart path immediately after confirmation. No editing gap may exist between confirmation and activation. On the next running app instance, report the actual durable outcome once using existing feedback and clear staging state. Pre-activation refusal says “The backup was not applied: <actionable reason>. Your current data is unchanged.” Interrupted recovery reports the coherent state actually selected; never claim unchanged data if a validated new state has already committed.

**Alternative D7:** retain next-start activation, with persistent pending status on **every tab**, “Changes made now will be replaced”, Restart to restore and Cancel restore. Recents resume is not silently described as a process restart. Both alternatives retain the same validation/integrity/preservation/interruption requirements. A Back up first **button** from optional R1 is drawn as a proposed refinement using the existing backup path; base approval otherwise requires truthful advice only. Cancellation/refusal must retain the picked/current data appropriately and leave no false pending banner.

**Owners:** BackupSection/BackupViewModel, FileBackupService/BackupArchive/BackupStaging, main startup/AppDatabase, feedback helpers. **SP09.A+:** exact state/message/clear behavior and D7 UI, production-open refusal/content/FK/migration/interruption tests, applied/refused message-once widgets and literal G2 Recents/phone rehearsal. Prototype success/refusal screens prove no data safety.

### SP10 — Records, visible deletion and acquisition export

**Owner amendment AM-01 (2026-10-18):** AM-01 cards visibly distinguish Result not recorded, Completed, Partly and Not done, with direct Record/Edit result and planned/recorded integration. The user can explicitly report completion for the current saved night before dawn; future-night acquisition stays refused and nothing auto-completes. Domain/repository/UI all follow this rule using the saved context. Saved-changed records resolve Save/Discard/Cancel before snapshot review; preserve all transactional, stale-form, historical-event and derived-state protections. Astronomical night/rollover timing remains unchanged.

**Sources:** PD-19/Q4/RD-07/RD-09, P2-F13/COV-01; F19/F20. Logbook has Entries/Progress by target; Library manages rigs/targets/sites. Target row exposes Plan this target. Entry shows the stored forecast values/source/age from its snapshot, with absent/unreadable legacy state explicit, never today's weather substituted. Copy/record-result/reopen/progress retain originals and manual-reported-result semantics.

Stored rig/target/site/entry Delete is visible and uses the current destructive confirmation; Cancel/Back/tap outside retains it. Capture block edits and generated-calibration changes retain edit Undo. No stored-record Undo is added. CSV uses only supported recorded acquisition data under the refreshed receiving contract; absent data is unavailable/blank, never fabricated from the plan. Numeric filter IDs stay blank, per-filter light rows remain distinct, ordered filter names accompany share output, and calibration counts require supported recorded acquisition evidence **and** proven whole-tuple coverage (TF-01); a planned/library block alone does not establish a count. Optional R12 proposes displaying that ordered map in-app as well; no ID lookup, account or upload integration.

**Owners:** Logbook/Progress/SessionDetail/Results/selection screens, sessions/results/lifecycle VMs, immutable snapshots/reconciliation/repository, existing manifest/share/export. **SP10.A+:** COV-01 correction; explicit F19 segment/target-row/RD-07 outcomes and result-state/snapshot+derived-UI tests; F20 no-result/mixed/status/legacy/group/CSV escaping/unknown fixtures. Optional extra mapping UI remains separately dispositioned.

### SP11 — Effective assumptions with a clear Settings scope

**Owner amendment AM-03 (2026-10-18):** the place-name lookup switch follows the same honest-persistence rule as AM-02's settings: after a failed write it never shows a choice different from the stored one without an unsaved cue and Retry, or a stated restore; nothing is sent while Off is shown. Default, consent wording and provider are unchanged.

**Owner amendment AM-02 (2026-10-18):** AM-02 persistence behavior keeps latest edits visibly unsaved on failure, with Retry of the newest complete configuration; no false saved acknowledgement or silent mixed configuration after restart. Compatible storage/concurrency implementation stays local; privacy opt-in behavior is unchanged.

**Owner amendment AM-01 (2026-10-18):** AM-01 Settings is grouped as basic planning, collapsed advanced constraints/overheads, guidance, display/privacy/data/About. Keep all existing meaningful effects and canonical values. Darkness/minimum altitude/frame interval/margin are basic; optional gates and dither/refocus/filter/flip/setup are advanced; NPF and dew are contextual guidance. Show effective assumptions near Capture Plan and explain global scope; Back returns to the same plan.

**Sources:** PD-10/14/20/21, K12, optional R3; F12/F21. Show only assumptions actually affecting the current result, retaining effective darkness/altitude/margin and accessible advanced settings. Correct “Change in Planning Settings” to “Change in Settings”. Make it clear these settings apply globally to current planning, while stored records retain their saved assumptions. Proposed R3 opens Settings above the current plan and returns to the same plan/section on Back; if not adopted, retain the existing tab behavior with explicit return guidance rather than pretending it was changed.

Appearance and unit display remain F21, with D1 unresolved; canonical scientific values do not change. Global interval stays global. Focus reminder has no numeric control or new calculation. Every evaluable block's what-fits wording comes from FitAnalyzer, and unknown inputs do not hide still-valid results.

**Owners:** CaptureAssumptionsPanel, SettingsViewModel/PlanningPreferences, AppRouter, shared QuantityText/theme/preference backup. **SP11.A+:** effective-value assertions, fit-partial states, navigation/cancel and frozen-snapshot distinction; separate absolute versus difference conversions. Optional return-path refinement does not rewrite saved records.

## 21. Proposed capture and night contracts for the local ADR checkpoints

These are concrete recommendations ready for technical review, **not accepted ADR-021/022 or schema implementation**. Technical encoding and the domain vectors are finalized in the existing F03/F13 checkpoints; no extra research stage is introduced. Behavior is bounded here so P4 can identify exact contradictions and P5 can ask concrete choices.

### CP contract — F03/F04/F06/F07/F08/F09/F10/F11

| Field / association | Proposed semantic contract | Round-trip / negative check |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Block parameters | Retain CaptureBlock bounds/type/class rules: finite positive exposure, existing count/binning/filter limits, sensitivity kind distinct. No new physical controls/nullable rig model | Existing constructor/form limits, type-switch, class-inapplicable preserved historical values and Cancel |
| Per-field origin (D5) | Nullable field-keyed record: kind entered / copied from block / inherited / file / explicitly declared; immutable display descriptor and applicable source/evidence reference. Missing legacy record means origin unrecorded, never entered/file by default | Switch/edit/reorder/delete-Undo/copy/save/reopen/snapshot/backup; user edit replaces only that field's origin. Source block edit/deletion must not retroactively change copied values or invalidate history |
| Origin references | Block/file labels describe the proposal at the time it was accepted, not a live calculation dependency. Remap copied block references where applicable; retain a descriptive origin if the source no longer exists. No dangling FK may make a historical snapshot unreadable | Delete/rename/source-edit/copy then reopen shows the same copied value and honest descriptor. Do not implement a reactive inheritance graph |
| Metadata/source privacy | Preserve tag/provenance/format meaning and known basename/source label where already approved. No absolute path/URI, raw header dump, inferred GPS or extra sensor/control introduced merely to store origin | Invalid/unknown/conflicting fields explicit; bounded reader limits; exported/source descriptors contain only approved data |
| White balance | Optional Auto/named preset/Kelvin/Custom with optional tint for Phone/DSLR/Unknown; file Auto/Manual and DNG neutral vector remain distinct typed evidence. No neutral-vector→Kelvin conversion. Legacy unrecorded | Save/reopen/entry/share/additive manifest/backup; WB must leave exposure/budget/storage unchanged. Astro controls hidden, retained historic records not erased |
| Measured file-size evidence (D3/D5) | Positive byte length, format/read status, source label and known dimensions/layout/compression/binning/mode association. Measured compatible output wins; mode change invalidates or explicitly reconfirms. Do not scale measured bytes by binning | Already-binned/unbinned measured vectors, changed output mode, multiple-file pick order, block/rig association and preserved snapshots |
| Recognized but unread file (D3) | Recommended: user-selected one-file byte length may estimate one frame, explicitly “mode not read; compressed sizes vary with content”; it supplies no dimensions/compression. Manual-size remedy remains. Alternative: refuse this branch with reason and manual action | Synthetic CR3 byte-length→decimal MB; no phantom tag values; unread mode cannot support theoretical payload |
| Theoretical payload (D4) | Only evidenced supported uncompressed DNG/FITS layout or explicit confirmed declared format. Normalize known native/output geometry once; ambiguous writer conventions remain unsupported/Unknown. Distinguish pixel payload from container/padding | Source S1–S4 and independent integer/float/FITS-padding/compression/binning vectors; no class-derived bit depth or compression ratio |
| Schema/snapshot/manifest | One compatible v25→v26 migration for accepted nullable fields/associations; additive snapshot/manifest keys under existing version contract. Missing/future-unknown values parse safely without invented origins. Old snapshots/results/forecasts remain immutable | Schema equality + content and derived-state comparisons, all named legacy/result/alias/preferences preserved; backup/manifest round trips and newer-schema data-safe refusal |
| FITS reuse | One registry/typed contract into EquipmentCandidate→match→review→Save and approved block fields; geometry keyword units and actual-vs-setpoint/gain kinds explicit. No hardware control or decoder expansion | END/read/axis/overflow/conflict/asymmetry fixtures plus real licensed/owner frame and device row; missing file blocks only the named FITS scope/final close |
| Calibration identity/coverage | Existing class applicability; whole modeled tuple, trimmed/case-folded filter equality, no alias guessing. Unknown advisory compatibility never proves known-value duplicate coverage. Counts/exposure examples are proposals only | Ha/bin1/OIII/bin2/flat-Ha-bin2, HA/Ha/H-alpha, known/unknown sensitivity, LENR, library/timing, preview/Cancel/Undo and budget invariance |
| O2b comparability | T=N·t and normalized ideal √(T/Tplan), separate known incompatible sensitivity/binning groups or explicitly label approved idealization; unknown does not prove equivalence. Empty/zero group has text, not a fabricated gain | Independent R-O2 values/tolerance from the existing source; zero/mixed/unknown groups; axes/reference point/text and no absolute-SNR claim |

Exact column/JSON names, IDs and future-enum encoding are engineering decisions in the accepted ADR, not a product choice silently settled by this table. D5 needs explicit persistence association acceptance; neither a screenshot nor a recommendation grants it. Domain limits and source-vector tolerance remain those of the repository, not newly invented here.

### NC contract — F13/F14/F15 plus navigation callers

| Item | Proposed semantic contract | Check / boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| MF-c / coordinate frame | One semantic Moon-up definition everywhere; derive topocentric sampling equivalence to geocentric h₀, including equality/refraction/upper-limb assumptions. Do not copy a geocentric threshold beside a different-frame altitude | ADR-022 derivation, independent SI-015/boundary/polar/reference vectors and batch=single. Sketches do not supply the derivation |
| Moon-free intervals | Intersect the selected SessionNight's dark interval with Moon-below intervals. Distinguish evening/morning/all-night/none/mixed-disjoint/no-dark and missing-input states. No-dark is known absence of darkness, not the same as missing weather or an unknown value | Interval vectors including high latitude/no rise/set; same values in details/calendar/gate/text; phase never substitutes for interval calculation |
| Phase and calendar | Name phase from phase longitude at the existing model's defined reference instant; ADR names it explicitly. Offline month computation in isolate, cached by relevant site/zone/night-definition/model inputs; large-text week rendering and field hatching/text legend | Calendar/detail agreement and cache invalidation; physical timing measured at the existing gate. No Moonlit implementation reuse or universal good-night threshold |
| Weather time/coverage | Slice actual UTC hours for each resolved site night and selected dark span. Preserve instantaneous vs preceding-hour semantics; freeze partial-hour/dew-duration conventions in ADR-022. Up to 16 returned days is not 16 complete nights | First/last partial, absent-variable/no-dark/DST/out-of-range/stale/offline vectors; unknown never becomes zero/clear |
| Weather cache/races | Identity includes relevant coordinates/model/range/version, not site ID alone. Reuse/migrate/drop old keys safely; a late response cannot attach to a new edited site/night | Same-ID coordinate changes/out-of-order results/legacy key cleanup and correct stored forecast association |
| Two time labels | Retrieval age says how old this snapshot is; lead time says how far its forecasted night is from its source snapshot, under the exact ADR-022 rounding convention. They are not a reliability score | Source/age/lead labels and boundary fixtures; 1/2/3-day labels in sketches are illustrative only |
| Browse/adopt D9 | Browse date/month/forecast card is read-only; explicitly label viewed and active-plan context. Home/Planner editing remains current behavior under the recommended option. Adoption calls existing guarded draft/copy paths; saved originals/results are immutable | Control/state matrix SP08; contents **and derived UI** after Cancel/Back/restart/copy/site change; no second plan store or implicit retiming |
| Providers/privacy | Same Open-Meteo data and opt-in existing services; Clear Outside only on explicit user link. Update actual request/privacy/COMPLIANCE together when implemented | Call budget/live G1 checks, notices/policy consistency and no new seeing/provider/score |

The unresolved local derivation/encoding/rounding items are the already scheduled technical ADR checkpoints, not promises that no technical decision remains. Their dependent implementation cannot claim completion until accepted and evidenced. They do not block independent restore-safety or startup correction work.

## 22. Simplification dispositions and compatibility

KEEP retains a capability; MOVE/SIMPLIFY changes prominence without deleting data or scientific behavior. REMOVE FROM UI is limited to the two explicitly approved actions. No capability removal is recommended as an automatic consequence of a clean layout.

| Candidate | Disposition / proposed replacement | Distinct utility and compatibility | Authority / state |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Integration/budget/fit/scientific inputs | KEEP with effective summaries and one-level detail | Core planning answer, snapshots/export and existing calculations remain | Approved planning-first/PD-05/06/14; SP03/SP11 |
| Detailed weather | MOVE TO DETAILS, keep factual Home card | Supports conditions and stored forecast; no new provider or score | Q1/PD-16/18; SP02/SP07 |
| Candidates and altitude chart | SIMPLIFY prominence: secondary candidates, compact chart | Different target selection/window reasoning utilities remain | Q1; SP02/SP03 |
| Repeated context/empty target | MERGE repeated statements into checklist/one context | Keep site/night/zone; do not conflate browse and edit | PD-03/04, D9 pending; SP02/SP03/SP08 |
| Home New plan button | REMOVE FROM UI on Home only; retain Planner menu | New plan resets to a different draft; Open planner resumes the current one. They are not lifecycle duplicates | PD-03, existing Save/Discard/Cancel; SP02 |
| Example Capture Plan action | REMOVE FROM UI only after real replacements pass | Keep ExampleCapturePlan fixture and E2E migration; do not remove calculations or seed data | PD-13; F09 A5/A6; SP03/SP06 |
| Repeated identical tracking warning | MERGE into one visible contextual warning where applicable | Keep differing per-block reasons/conflicts named and reachable; no silent warning removal | Proposed density refinement within M11, owner visual review; SP03 |
| Source/WB/storage explanation density | MOVE TO DETAILS with short visible source/Unknown/conflict | Field values/origins survive all operations, exports and backups; critical unknowns/conflicts not hidden | PD-08/09, D3/D5 pending; optional R9 layout; SP05 |
| Calibration entry | KEEP, make labelled and reachable | Uses existing modeled matching and preview, not a new library subsystem | PD-12; optional R8 shortcut separately proposed; SP06 |
| Skip and redundant setup target step | REMOVE FROM UI, keep one Start planning | Optional site/rig setup remains; target selection stays in planner/library | PD-02; SP01 |
| Assumptions and advanced controls | SIMPLIFY summary; KEEP every applicable control in Settings | Do not change global overhead model or recorded snapshot values | PD-14/21; SP11 |
| Stored-record Delete vs block Undo | KEEP each distinct contract; correct F19 wording | Stored records confirm first; block edits use Undo. No new restore/record Undo | RD-09; COV-01, D2 pending; SP09/SP10 |
| RAW decoding, partial-rig model, new providers, absolute SNR, numeric focus | No new capability / preserve explicit exclusions | No data/model expansion masquerading as simplification | Existing exclusions; only explicit future owner amendment could change them |
| Working science, saved snapshots/results or export history | **No REMOVE CAPABILITY** | Layout changes must preserve accessible values and data compatibility | Existing invariants/PD decisions |

## 24. P3 exit and independent review handoff

P3 deliverables are complete: significant concrete sketches/states, one recommended outcome with material alternatives, named source→F/A/check/layer mappings, separate simplification/data-compatibility dispositions, and finite owner questions. All sketches remain PROPOSED. No owner decision, roadmap authority, production code, normal test/tooling input or pre-existing change was modified.

Next is one P4 feasibility pass over the current roadmap and §19–§23, with earlier matrices read only as needed for an exact source. The workflow's opening says the model-role distribution is a **recommendation**; independence and truthful attribution are the required process properties. Claude is the recommended reviewer. A different available independent reviewer may be identified explicitly; never label it Claude or a human and never present author self-review as independent. P1/P2 references to a separate Claude review describe that recommendation, not a tool capability or owner approval.

The reviewer must inspect actual contracts/dependencies/negative paths and return a finite disposition table, preserving all accepted outcomes and local ADR/resource checkpoints. It must not implement, create a competing queue, add product scope or re-run historical checks merely for freshness. Parent remains the sole roadmap/report editor. P5 then consolidates only actual findings and unresolved owner choices. P6/approval/start/release remain separate; this phase has not completed the overall goal.

## 25. Technical feasibility review

**P4 COMPLETE:** one read-only independent **Codex** review, fresh context, task `/root/p4_feasibility`, returned 2026-10-18. Claude CLI is unavailable; model-role substitution follows the workflow's recommendation wording, with explicit attribution. This is neither a Claude review nor human validation. The reviewer inspected current code, decisions, all F01–F26 and P1–P3, made no edits, ran no application checks, accessed no owner database/private samples/signing material and spawned no agents. The parent records the returned result here.

**Verdict:** all F01–F26 are technically feasible within the existing architecture, subject to their decisions, local contract checkpoints and mandatory external evidence. No new demonstrated architectural impossibility or dependency/release-chain cycle was found. One additional narrow clarification, TF-01, must be bound before freeze, alongside existing COV corrections. This is permission to advance to P5, not a READY-for-approval verdict or implementation/acceptance PASS.

| F26 A1–A5 | **Feasible after G1/signing prerequisites; current checker insufficient alone.** tool/check_bundle.dart targets nested AAB /lib entries, checks ELF alignment, accepts non-debug signer subject and skips signer verification when keytool absent. Qualify actual APK contents and fail closed on missing expected fingerprint/identity/version evidence. Build/measure candidates, obtain D6, check/hash selected artifacts. Changes to tooling/version invalidate affected final-input gate/CI evidence. A5 signature-mismatch instructions require a verified independent backup before separately authorized uninstall; no automated destructive shortcut or key access |

### TF-01 — Recorded acquisitions versus compatible calibration

**Disposition: must clarify before freeze**, a narrow criterion wording correction, not a demonstrated runtime CSV failure or new feature. F20 A1 forbids inventing unsupported acquisitions from a plan; A4 requires F09 whole-tuple coverage. ADR-019/manual reported results do not attest every planned calibration acquisition.

**CODE evidence:** `lib/data/repositories/drift_session_repository.dart`, `_reportCounts` explicitly skips non-light blocks; CompletedAsPlanned assigns planned counts only to lights. `lib/domain/services/calibration_match.dart`, `CalibrationMatch.matches` establishes settings compatibility, not acquisition occurrence. Merely exporting planned matching flats/darks/bias after a completed-light report would invent counts; a library calibration block is an especially clear negative case.

**Minimum correction to F20 A4/SP10/CHK-F20.A4:** “Calibration counts are exported only when supported recorded acquisition evidence exists and F09 proves the required whole-tuple association. A planned block or a compatible library block does not establish an acquired count; otherwise leave the field blank.” F09 remains the compatibility provider; no expansion of F19 result-entry scope is required.

**Planned check:** manual completed/partial-light result with compatible planned calibration → blank calibration counts; supported historical recorded calibration with proven tuple → recorded count; mismatched/unknown tuple, unrecorded or library-only plan → blank. These are expected fixtures, not executed CSV tests. TF-01 is an A1/A4 consistency clarification, requiring no new owner product vote.

| ID | P6 binding / objective closure |
| --- | --- |
| COV-01 | F19 scope/A4/verification follows RD-09: visible Delete→confirm→delete, Cancel preserves record/selection/snapshot. Keep intervening-edit Undo checks in F06/F09 |
| COV-02 | Bind each exact source-only outcome in explicit F criteria/frozen clause appendix, using §23 owners/checks. F25 alone is not behavioral coverage |
| COV-03 | F10 conditional for F18 FITS rows; bound F24 prerequisites before F25, excluding F25/F26. Graph with FITS unavailable must leave unrelated work eligible |

| COV-07 | Incorporate accepted P3/owner answers into scheduled F03/F13 contract checkpoints. ADRs remain Planned; proposals/reviewer recommendations are not approval |

### Local implementation details, not new freeze blockers

1. F01 activation journal representation, coherent database/preferences switch, live connection closure/sidecars and outcome-message-once are engineering obligations under A1–A5. “Your data is unchanged” is suitable for refusal before activation, not every interrupted recovery after a new state committed. Check each mutation boundary and actual durable outcome.
2. F03/F06 nullable-update representation and origin-descriptor copy/remapping must cover constructors/reconstruction/source deletion/revert/stale Undo/export/backup. No live inheritance graph or dangling mandatory FK. These are consequences of accepted semantics, not a new persistence architecture decision.
3. F07 per-block evidence resolution and budget-cache invalidation must follow actual output mode. Same length/different mode and already-binned cases check this. F09's permissive advice and positive coverage may use separate predicates/results; globally tightening sameSensitivity would risk changing approved Unknown advice.
4. F13–F15 month iteration/reference instant/forecast partial hours/dew duration/rounding are named local ADR details. ADR-007 retains site evening-date identity and exactly24-hour mean-solar-noon UTC windows; DST changes labels/offsets, not SessionNight.length. Check DST/high latitude/fractional longitude/no-dark separately.
5. F05/F16 propagate caller and rig identity, select exactly once where D8 permits, preserve Cancel and Library-only-save. F26 may reuse ELF parsing, but needs actual APK entry/signer checks; absent/debug/wrong signer, missing verifier and expected signer are meaningful cases. No preference for an API design becomes a blocker.

No local item requires another global research stage, new provider, second metadata/persistence system or changed scientific scope. Optional R1/R3/R6/R8/R9/R11/R12 remain optional. D1–D9 are unresolved; scientific/encoding acceptance and physical resources are gates, not assumed achievements.

### Verified dependency interpretation and release evidence boundaries

The intended **G1→F26→G2→G3→G4** chain is acyclic and feasible. G1 freezes finite acceptance rows and qualifies F01–F25 with carried phone rows named. F26 may change packaging tooling/version inputs; its A3 then needs valid checks/native evidence and remote CI on final release inputs. A G1 PASS does not automatically cover a subsequent relevant change. Public CI need not receive private release keys.

Owner-managed signing supplies measurement candidates; D6 follows actual universal/arm64 measurements. Verify identity, expected public signer fingerprint, versionCode, alignment and SHA-256 on every selected APK. F26 size measurement is not G2 acceptance. G2 qualifies those exact hashes by clean install/same-key update and isolated recovery/migration rehearsals. A signed lower-versionCode update precursor is not the release candidate. G3 reconciles rows and records owner go/no-go; G4 publishes the tested bytes, downloads/hash-compares and performs the specified loop. Rebuild means new candidate and invalidates affected artifact evidence.

Real DNG/JPEG/HEIC, licensed/owner FITS, actual phone/native launch/IME/process death, owner visual acceptance, external signer/authorized remote CI/live providers and publication remain prerequisites. No private resource was accessed. Missing FITS isolates its dependent scope; missing mandatory phone evidence prevents release readiness; accepted independent-usability limitation stays separately labelled.

**P4 exit:** proceed to P5, carrying TF-01 and known COV bindings into P6. Do not mark any F implemented, any gate passed or the proposal approved. No second comprehensive review is requested; corrections/rechecks are bounded by V5–V7.

## 26. Owner decision package — P5

**P5 COMPLETE — ready for explicit owner answers. No choice below is accepted.** This incorporates the actual P4 result (§25): technically feasible, with existing COV bindings and TF-01's acquisition-evidence clarification to apply in P6. This package concerns decisions and documentation preparation; it is not approval of a final roadmap version, an implementation start, permission to commit/push, or publication authorization. Silence and a recommended option are not decisions. The retained local ADR-021/022 acceptance checkpoints remain necessary for their exact encodings/reference vectors.

### Already accepted — no new vote

The current direction is planning-first, offline-capable, with Provider/ViewModels, go_router and Drift/SQLite, truthful Unknown/provenance and immutable saved history. RA.D/DECISIONS E.1, M1–M16, AUD-01–17 and approved Stage clauses retain their authority (§2–§7). The R3-final identity and held splash remain accepted; F02 corrects the pre-Android-12 failure, and reduced-motion/timing evidence remains outstanding. The working calculations and accessible detail are retained.

Previously approved but undelivered **calendar/combined Moon interval bar, recorded white balance, CSV/results/share fields, calibration preview and night overview** remain completion scope. “No new features” excludes unapproved expansion; it does not delete those outcomes, and it does not mean that documenting them proves implementation. Whole-tuple calibration coverage, source review/Cancel, measured-versus-theoretical storage, relative stacking gain rather than absolute SNR, records/saved-plan safety, and finite release rows remain required. P6 will bind the exact COV-02 outcomes into A-criteria instead of asking the owner to vote again.

RD-09 already chooses confirmation before deleting stored rigs/targets and Undo for in-plan edits; correcting F19 is transcription, not a new Undo decision. COV-03's conditional FITS dependency and F24 ordering are also plan corrections. Neither a missing FITS sample nor an unresolved optional UI recommendation stops independent authorized work. No capability is proposed for removal (§22).

## 31. AM-01 owner-authorized plan amendment (2026-10-18)

**Owner authorization:** “Я згоден з твоїми зауваженнями, внось корективи в план” [“I agree with your observations; amend the plan”], followed by the emphasis that Capture Plan is a simple primary feature: enter intended capture, see approximate SNR/storage/time, and plan. The owner also requested a bounded adjacent functions/UI/UX assessment and conclusions **before** deciding whether to add those new findings. This authorizes the preceding seven-point assessment's documentation changes; it does not authorize application changes or silently promote the new findings.

**Recorded interpretation:** simplicity concerns the entry structure and presentation, not deletion of valid scientific/calculation behavior. Existing comparable-frame relative stacking gain supplies an honest estimate; absolute physical SNR still lacks the necessary accepted inputs/model and is neither invented nor labelled as implemented. Time separates light integration from estimated total duration; storage remains measured/theoretical/Unknown with source. Detailed calculations, original snapshots/results, units, provenance and negative-path safety remain accessible and unchanged in intent.

The normative AM-01 source→task→A→check table is in the roadmap §4. Changes: F06 A5; F11 A3; F12 A4; F14 A3; F15 A1/new A5; F18 A1; F19 A1/A2/new A5; F23/new A5. Existing 26 F IDs/aliases remain, with118 A/check rows. F19's changed persisted result eligibility is H/high-risk for future implementation. F15's cursor is transient viewing only; F23 keeps existing consent/provider/privacy and draft-only proposals. No new engine, score, parser, partial-rig model, tracker or provider was approved.

**Version/history:** base P8 -02 and P7's six-point verdict remain historical evidence for that exact version. Current owner-authorized amendment is P6-2026-10-18-03. §20's amended SP contracts and §4–§10 source ledger are hashed again in the roadmap. Historical clauses are preserved with explicit AM-01 precedence; no original approval/hash is retroactively rewritten. New adjacent findings in §32 are not execution tasks.

**References:** all active project paths are relative; `docs/Examples/README.md` indexes supplied images/Moonlit and the unmodified attached slider reference. External source filenames/hashes describe provenance, not required personal directories. Raw historical logs/patches retain authentic original host details and are not normative project paths.

Stage A remains next, with no F task implemented or accepted by documentation. Existing no-commit/no-push/no-owner-data/no-secret/no-publication boundaries remain. CODE scenario, not executed reproduction | Check current query/request generation before accepting success/error; debounce is optional. Existing runWithFeedback reports failure, but displayed applied values can outlive a failed save without an explicit pending/unsaved distinction. No performance failure or torn write was measured | Decide whether to keep/retry unsaved values or roll back; do not silently assert persistence. Test injected save failure and reload, rapid updates with delayed writes, newest accepted value and no lost unrelated settings. Commit-on-drag-end/serialization is an engineering option only after evidence. **OWNER BEHAVIOR CHOICE / TEST NEEDED** |
| UX32-05 / CONTEXT USABILITY | Planner target card shows Altitude now alongside the selected plan's night and Max altitude in windows (`home_screen.dart`). The label is accurate, so this is not a calculation bug; for a future-night plan it mixes observation-now and planning-time concepts on the primary form | Lead with selected-night useful information; move explicitly labelled current-sky altitude into detail when irrelevant. ### Assessment conclusion and boundary

Prioritize completing the simple capture-entry/output/Save loop and its accepted safety contracts. The strongest additional functional candidate is the out-of-order target search; the most visible usability candidate is the disappearing Save action. Settings failure feedback deserves a deterministic test before choosing behavior. Label-scope and current-versus-planned metrics can be assessed together in the existing W walkthrough. These recommendations are deliberately not applied to app code or the execution criteria; the owner requested conclusions first for new findings.
