### 34.4 New findings register (proposals; not scope)

**Later — owner selection AM-03 (2026-10-18):** CRUX-01–CRUX-04 were selected into the plan (roadmap §4 AM-03; §35 below), with the Home title as the separate open choice AM03-T. CRUX-05 and CRUX-06 remain proposals. The records below keep their review-time dispositions and evidence levels.

**CRUX-01 — "Tonight" wording describes the plan's selected night.** USABILITY (context labelling; calculations correct).
- Goal and state: plan ahead (the planning-first use) or copy a plan to another night; plan night Fri, Dec 18 while the current night is Dec 15.
- Expected: wording names the plan's night (or "this night") whenever it is not tonight; Home's title and candidate action agree with the night shown.
- Actual: HOST — Planner status "Fill tonight's window: L 60 s × 396", block row "+296 frames still fit tonight"; Home title "Tonight" above "Fri, Dec 18" and "What can I image tonight?", which lists candidates for Dec 18. CODE — the same pattern in `plan_status.dart` (`targetReason`, `FillWindowAction`: "Trim … to fit tonight", "Not even one … fits tonight"), `block_text.dart` (`whatFits`), `home_screen.dart` ("no window tonight", "Set a site to see tonight's altitude chart."), `capture_analysis_viewmodel.dart` (`_computeFit` reasons), `opportunity_text.dart`, `sky_darkness_widget.dart`, `night_text.dart`/`tonight_home_screen.dart` ("not tonight", "no dark time tonight"), `tonight_candidates_screen.dart` ("Tonight's candidates") and domain `fit_analyzer.dart` reason strings.
- Cause: copy hard-codes "tonight" while `NightConditionsViewModel`, `CaptureAnalysisViewModel` and `CandidatesViewModel` compute for `SessionPlanViewModel.sessionNight`.
- Impact: a user cannot tell whether the fit, spare frames and candidates refer to today or to the planned night, on every plan made ahead.
- Coverage: partial. F18 A1 requires selected-plan-night facts to lead but names only Altitude now; F17 A1/A3 keep Home's card on the plan's night but keep the "Tonight" title (SP02). No criterion or check covers this wording.
- Minimal recommendation: one shared night phrase — "tonight" only when the plan's SessionNight is the current night, otherwise the evening date — applied to the listed strings (domain reasons via presentation mapping or a passed phrase); no calculation change.
- Closure check: a widget test with plan night ≠ current night finds no "tonight" in Planner, Home and Candidates and finds the date; the tonight case unchanged.
- Owner decision: whether Home keeps the title "Tonight" when the plan's night differs (keep the tab name and show the plan's night in the header, or title by the night).

**CRUX-02 — A failed place-name opt-out returns after restart.** POTENTIAL (privacy persistence; failure injected).
- Goal and state: the user turns off "Look up place names" after having turned it on; the privacy write fails.
- Expected: the switch shows the durable state or an explicit unsaved state with Retry; never Off on screen while On is stored.
- Actual: HOST — the switch reads Off and lookups stop for the session (by code, the screen then shows the generic "Couldn't save the place-name setting" message); after a reload the lookup is enabled again (`placeNameLookup` and the geocoder `enabled` both true), so positions are sent again without a visible change.
- Cause: `SettingsViewModel.setPlaceNameLookup` sets `OptInReverseGeocoder.enabled` and notifies before awaiting `PrivacyPreferencesRepository.savePlaceNameLookup`.
- Impact: rare, but it silently resumes a withdrawn data flow. A failed opt-in is harmless (it reverts to Off).
- Coverage: partial. F21 A5 covers planning and display/unit preferences and keeps privacy semantics; the place-name switch is not named.
- Minimal recommendation: apply F21 A5's honest-failure rule to this switch (keep Off visibly unsaved with Retry, or restore the durable state and say so); no change to the default, consent wording or provider.
- Closure check: injected failure for Off and for On, reload, the displayed state equals the stored one or is marked unsaved, no lookup while Off is shown.
- Owner decision: only whether to select it into F21 A5.

**CRUX-03 — A GPS fix overwrites coordinates typed after the tap.** DEFECT (async ordering).
- Goal and state: in the site editor, tap Use current position, then type (or pick on the map) while the fix is pending.
- Expected: a manual edit made after the tap wins, or the user chooses.
- Actual: HOST — typed latitude 10.00000 became 46.05000 when the fix arrived; the name stayed. Leaving the editor while pending is safe.
- Cause: `_SiteEditorScreenState._useCurrentPosition` writes both coordinate controllers on `LocationFound` unconditionally; the fields and "Pick on map" stay enabled while `_locating`.
- Impact: a wrong position can be saved if the change is not noticed (night, Moon and weather for the wrong place).
- Coverage: partial. F23 A5 says a lookup never overwrites a later name/position edit and ignores stale responses, written for the place-name proposal; the coordinate fill itself is not explicit.
- Minimal recommendation: use the fix only if the coordinate fields are unchanged since the tap (otherwise keep the user's values and offer the fix), and treat a map pick during locating the same way; clarify F23 A5's check to include it.
- Closure check: controlled GPS completer — untouched fields are filled; typed or map-picked values are kept; leaving writes nothing.
- Owner decision: none.

**CRUX-04 — A remembered position is labelled "Current position".** USABILITY (honest labelling).
- Goal and state: know which position the night is computed for; a transient position (GPS fix, map point, typed coordinates) with place-name lookup off (the default), remembered across restarts.
- Expected: a label that does not claim the live device position (for example "Unsaved position" with coordinates).
- Actual: HOST — Planner and Home show "Current position" for fixture coordinates set without GPS. CODE — the same fallback in `home_screen.dart`, `tonight_home_screen.dart`, `welcome_screen.dart` and `sites_screen.dart` (Sites adds the coordinates and "not saved").
- Impact: after travelling, or after a map pick, the user may believe the plan follows the device.
- Coverage: none specific (F17 A1 shapes the context; F23 A5 proposes names only in the new-site editor).
- Minimal recommendation: rename the fallback; show coordinates or "not saved" where space allows; no automatic GPS or behaviour change.
- Closure check: map-pick, typed and GPS fixtures before and after restart show the new label.
- Owner decision: the wording, if the owner wants to choose it.

**CRUX-05 — Adding another block is an icon-only control.** USABILITY (low).
- Goal and state: add a second block to a non-empty Capture Plan.
- Actual: CODE + render — a "+" `IconButton` with the tooltip "Add capture block" at the card's top right; no visible text. The empty state's only labelled action is the example (F09 A6 replaces it).
- Impact: discoverability of the core loop's entry; semantics are present through the tooltip.
- Coverage: F09 A6 (empty state) and F06 A7 (dialog label) do not cover the non-empty state.
- Minimal recommendation: a labelled "Add block" control (icon and text) consistent with F06 A7; U at 412 px/200 %.
- Owner decision: none.

**CRUX-06 — The example plan warns about itself.** VISUAL (low).
- Actual: HOST render — after "Start from the example", the flat (no filter, `ExampleCapturePlan`) shows "No light block uses this filter" with "Match the lights", and the plan shows "No flats for: L".
- Coverage: **covered by F09 A5/A6**, which retire the example action once the replacements work and keep the class as a test fixture. No new task; only if the action is kept for a release would the fixture's flat need the lights' filter (and its tests updated).

### 34.5 Separation of claims and capability dispositions

- **Confirmed behaviour (HOST, this probe):** UX32-01, UX32-03 (wrong row selectable; error replaces newer list), UX32-04 (shown versus durable value; torn write), UX32-05, CRUX-01, CRUX-03, CRUX-04, CRUX-06; CRUX-02 under an injected failure.
- **Code-based potential risk:** UX32-04's out-of-order overwrite with a real platform store; CRUX-02's real-world frequency.
- **Recommended improvement:** CRUX-05; CRUX-04's wording.
- **Unverified:** every DEVICE, 200 %/keyboard/theme/reduced-motion, human-comprehension, process-death, live-provider and backup-activation row; Night & Moon edge states at this HEAD.

No capability is proposed for removal by this review. Dispositions only restate accepted ones or the findings above: Altitude now — MOVE to labelled secondary detail (F18 A1); per-group gain graphs — MOVE to Details, values KEEP (F11 A3); "Tonight for this target" — REMOVE FROM UI with domain calculations kept (AM-01, F18 A1); Rig card NPF/max-sub/FOV/pixel-scale rows — SIMPLIFY into compact context, calculations KEEP (F18 A1); example action — REMOVE FROM UI after its replacements (F09); Home New plan — REMOVE FROM UI, Planner's kept (F17 A2); night wording and the position label — SIMPLIFY copy (CRUX-01, CRUX-04).

### 34.6 Conclusion and boundary

Ranked by concrete user impact: what most obstructs the simple Capture Plan is already planned — the disappearing Save (F18 A5), the long context before the blocks and the graphs before Save (F18 A1/F11 A3), and the block editor's scope, guard and filter behaviour (F06). UX32-03 is the most consequential confirmed defect (a wrong target can be chosen) and F24 A6 already covers it. Among new findings, CRUX-01 matters most for a planning-first app; CRUX-02 to CRUX-04 are bounded corrections to existing F18/F17, F21 and F23 contracts; CRUX-05 and CRUX-06 are low. None is proposed as a new release gate or freeze blocker. Missing evidence: a phone, 200 %/keyboard/theme renders of the current copy, a human walkthrough, process death and backup activation.

This review stops here: no implementation, no roadmap or PROGRESS change, no second queue, no request for another reviewer. Whether any CRUX item enters a later amendment is the owner's decision.

**Owner summary (Ukrainian, as the prompt requires; the English register above is the reference text).** Найбільше простому Capture Plan заважають уже відомі й заплановані речі: кнопка Save plan зникає, доки немає цілі, рига чи ночі (F18 A5); перед блоками кадрів багато контексту (ціль, риг із NPF, трекінг), а графіки виграшу стоять до збереження (F18 A1, F11 A3); у редакторі блоку «Add/Save» не відрізняється від збереження плану, а тап поза діалогом скидає зміни (F06). Гонку пошуку цілей (UX32-03) підтверджено: пізня відповідь на старий запит може підмінити список, і можна вибрати не ту ціль, — це вже покриває F24 A6. Нове, варте розгляду: (1) CRUX-01 — для плану на іншу ніч застосунок усе одно пише «tonight» (Fill tonight's window, What can I image tonight?), хоча рахує правильно для вибраної ночі; (2) CRUX-03 — відповідь GPS перезаписує координати, які ви вже ввели вручну; (3) CRUX-04 — збережена позиція підписана «Current position», хоча це може бути давня точка або точка з мапи; (4) CRUX-02 — якщо вимкнення пошуку назв місць не збереглося, після перезапуску пошук знову вмикається. CRUX-05 (кнопка «+» без підпису) і CRUX-06 (приклад плану показує попередження сам про себе) — дрібні; CRUX-06 зникне разом із прикладом у F09. Бракує доказів із телефона, рендерів 200 %/клавіатури/тем на поточному тексті, людського проходження, перевірки process death і відновлення з резервної копії.

### 34.7 Verification of this report

Documentation class (CLAUDE.md V1): the links and anchors added here and in the evidence README resolve; `git diff --check` and a whitespace scan of the new untracked files are clean; application inputs equal `322edbd` apart from the pre-existing macOS registrant; the probe lives in ignored `build/finalization/am02_review/` and its source is preserved as the evidence patch. No commit, push, gate, device or provider run.

## 36. AM03-T decided — Home title follows the plan's night

**Owner instruction, 2026-10-18:** «AM03-T: обираю варіант B (вкладка лишається Tonight, заголовок сторінки показує ніч плану). ## 38. Develop delivery and full-plan Claude goal execution handoff

The owner requested a commit/push to develop, followed by an implementation prompt covering the entire completed plan and a check of the goal-agent scenario. Baseline `2b467b8fd1478b6f8f0fcd48e887a88bc819ad8e` was pushed normally from previous remote develop `718c19b`; remote main was not updated. The canonical origin URL is now `https://github.com/Buffur/Culmina.git`. Current roadmap P6-2026-10-18-07, its fingerprint and 127 criteria remain as §37; no application implementation starts here.

The handoff is [`prompts/CLAUDE_FINAL_PLAN_GOAL_IMPLEMENTATION.md`](prompts/CLAUDE_FINAL_PLAN_GOAL_IMPLEMENTATION.md). Its 819-character goal command activates the bounded F01–F26/G1–G4 group only when submitted by the owner; it names task commits/ordinary develop pushes and separate owner/resource gates. CLAUDE and CONTINUE_CURRENT_WORK now resolve the single-task STOP versus finite-group continuation explicitly. PROGRESS records preparation, actual push and the start boundary. The old unconditional owner-app uninstall bullet is corrected to the existing backup/explicit-authorization rule, avoiding an unsafe instruction during autonomous release work.

| Scenario | Preparation result / limit |
| --- | --- |
| Fresh checkout or resumed session | Reads current PROGRESS and exact task/CHK/ADR; no old chat, host path or external workflow-file dependency; does not replay passed tasks |
| Finite whole-plan scope | All 26 F tasks and four gates mapped; current 127 criteria stay in one canonical register; continuation checkpoint inside launched group, no new global audit |
| More than one Claude agent | One lead/integration writer; helpers read/reproduce/review, coding only with isolation and stable ownership; no concurrent writes to develop, migration or PROGRESS |
| Failed checks or missing evidence | Meaningful regression and V1–V8; no fake PASS, observation-test fix claim, repeated unchanged gates or completion with mandatory evidence missing |
| Human/resource checkpoint | Concrete ADR-021/022/visual/D6/file/device/signing/release material; continue independent work, then truthful BLOCKED handoff rather than self-approval or spinning |
| Git/CI | Normal develop pushes, no main/force; develop now triggers unchanged CI gate steps. Baseline run [39124553324](https://github.com/Buffur/Culmina/actions/runs/39124553324) was observed in progress; no PASS inferred |
| Runtime goal | Official Claude goal/agent-team references checked 2026-10-18; goal condition fits the documented size. No Claude CLI on the preparing PATH; no actual Claude run, permission bypass or runtime acceptance claim |
| Final success | Every mandatory F/G condition including separately authorized published artifact must have actual evidence; a blocked handoff explicitly does not mean project completion |

All authored source links, task IDs and criterion bindings are checked; staged whitespace is checked with the immutable source/history/raw-log exceptions recorded in §37. CI trigger-only syntax/selection checked without rerunning a local full application gate. The goal/evidence evaluator is not an independent owner/scientific/device reviewer. No key, owner database, live backup activation, app install/uninstall, F code, release tag or publication was performed.
